\section*{T3 intermediate-EFT one-loop RGE term comparison: EFT\_1 after $F$}
This stage has active field content SM+$S_1+S_2$. The report combines the renormalisable RGBeta running with the dimension-five $\psi^2\phi^2$ Wilson-coefficient running generated after integrating out $F$.
\medskip
Throughout, $16\pi^2\,\mu\,dX/d\mu=\beta_X^{(1)}$. For the Wilson sector only the tree-generated stage-1 coefficient $C^{(0)}$ is inserted into the one-loop anomalous dimension; the one-loop threshold piece is retained as a boundary term and is not run again at this order.
\section*{Coupling and interaction notation}
\begin{longtable}{@{}p{0.23\linewidth}p{0.69\linewidth}@{}}
\toprule
symbol & interaction / definition \\
\midrule
$g_1$ & $D_\mu\supset i g_1YB_\mu$ \\
$g_2$ & $D_\mu\supset i g_2T^AW_\mu^A$ \\
$g_3$ & $D_\mu\supset i g_3t^AG_\mu^A$ \\
$Y_u$ & $-\bar QY_u\widetilde Hu+\mathrm{h.c.}$ \\
$Y_d$ & $-\bar QY_dHd+\mathrm{h.c.}$ \\
$Y_e$ & $-\bar LY_eHe+\mathrm{h.c.}$ \\
$y_1$ & $LFS_1+\mathrm{h.c.}$ \\
$y_2$ & $LFS_2+\mathrm{h.c.}$ \\
$M_F$ & $-\bar FM_FF$ \\
$T$ & $\operatorname{Tr}(Y_e^\dagger Y_e+3Y_u^\dagger Y_u+3Y_d^\dagger Y_d)$ \\
$T_\nu^{(1)}$ & $\operatorname{Tr}(y_1^\dagger y_1)$ \\
$T_\nu^{(2)}$ & $\operatorname{Tr}(y_2^\dagger y_2)$ \\
$T_\nu$ & $T_\nu^{(1)}+T_\nu^{(2)}$ \\
$m_1^2$ & $m_1^2S_1^\dagger S_1$ \\
$m_2^2$ & $m_2^2S_2^\dagger S_2$ \\
$\lambda_1$ & $\frac12\lambda_1(H^\dagger H)^2$ \\
$\lambda_{S_1}^{(1)}$ & $\frac12\lambda_{S_1}^{(1)}(S_1^\dagger S_1)^2$ \\
$\lambda_{S_2}^{(1)}$ & $\frac12\lambda_{S_2}^{(1)}(S_2^\dagger S_2)^2$ \\
$\lambda_{HS_1}^{(1)}$ & $\lambda_{HS_1}^{(1)}(H^\dagger H)(S_1^\dagger S_1)$ \\
$\lambda_{HS_2}^{(1)}$ & $\lambda_{HS_2}^{(1)}(H^\dagger H)(S_2^\dagger S_2)$ \\
$\lambda_{HS_1}^{(A)}$ & $\lambda_{HS_1}^{(A)}(H^\dagger T^AH)(S_1^\dagger T^AS_1)$ \\
$\lambda_{HS_2}^{(A)}$ & $\lambda_{HS_2}^{(A)}(H^\dagger T^AH)(S_2^\dagger T^AS_2)$ \\
$\lambda_5$ & $\lambda_5HHS_1S_2^\dagger+\mathrm{h.c.}$ \\
$\lambda_{12}^{(1)}$ & $\lambda_{12}^{(1)}(S_1^\dagger S_1)(S_2^\dagger S_2)$ \\
$\lambda_{12}^{(A)}$ & $\lambda_{12}^{(A)}(S_1^\dagger T^AS_1)(S_2^\dagger T^AS_2)$ \\
$\lambda_{12}^{(\times)}$ & $\lambda_{12}^{(\times)}(S_1^\dagger S_2)(S_2^\dagger S_1)$ \\
$\lambda_{S_1}^{(A)}$ & $\lambda_{S_1}^{(A)}(S_1^\dagger T^A S_1)(S_1^\dagger T^A S_1)$ \\
$\lambda_{S_2}^{(A)}$ & $\lambda_{S_2}^{(A)}(S_2^\dagger T^A S_2)(S_2^\dagger T^A S_2)$ \\
$\alpha$ & $Y_{S_1}=\alpha/2,\ Y_F=(\alpha+1)/2,\ Y_{S_2}=(\alpha+2)/2$ \\
$C_2(d)$ & $(d^2-1)/4$ \\
$T(d)$ & $d(d^2-1)/12$ \\
$C_{LLS_1S_2}^{ij;AB}$ & $L_iL_jS_1^AS_2^B$ \\
$C_{ijab}$ & $L_iL_j\phi_a\phi_b$ \\
$C_5^{ij}$ & $(L_i^TCL_j)HH$ \\
$R_W$ & $16\pi^2\beta_{C_5}\supset R_W\lambda_5C_{LLS_1S_2}$ \\
\bottomrule
\end{longtable}

\section*{Dimension-five $\psi^2\phi^2$ Wilson coefficients}
The Wilson tensor is reported in the real-scalar component basis $C_{ijab}$. Each component with a nonzero one-loop beta function has its own table. Components absent at the matching boundary but generated by operator mixing are included automatically.
\textit{No successful EFT1 Wilson-RGE results are available.}
